Let's think of LP boosting method. Could be more robust for off-center. It solves max sum g s.t. g≥0, (f*g)_k ≤ M for selected lags?
```
def proj_simplex(v, target_sum=1): # closed form
   u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
   cov = np.cumsum(u)
   rho = np.nonzero(u * np.arange(1,len(v)+1) > (cov[-1] - target_sum))[0][-1]
   theta = (cov[rho-1] - target_sum)/rho if rho>0 else 0
   out = np.maximum(v - theta,0); out/=target_sum/out.sum()? Actually projection to sum=target: max(v-theta,0) sum target. Then normalize again.
```

Let's brainstorm better off optimizer.

Objective off = max_{k≠n-1} c_k, S fixed. This is like minimizing the largest coefficient of a power series A(x)^2 except the middle coefficient. Let A(x)=∑a_i x^i. The off coefficients are all except x^{n-1}. We want all coefficients as equal/small. This resembles designing a polynomial with nonnegative coefficients, fixed sum, minimizing max coefficient of A^2 except middle. This is a polynomial/number-theory problem. Maybe can use linear programming over coefficients of A^2? The coefficients c_k = convolution. We want them ≤M. Want maximize sum a given M. This is exactly original LP! For maximum possible sum for fixed M: maximize S = ∑a_i subject to a≥0 and c_k≤M for all k off, and maybe scale. The score = M/S^2 (times 2n). The optimum sequence solves a convex feasibility? The set {a≥0: c_k≤M} is not convex because c_k quadratic. But if we fix M as variable and scale? Actually c_k are quadratic, constraints nonconvex. But the LP state-of-art with f*g linear approximates? Wait if we linearize c_k using current f? Not.

But there is a dual: maximize S^2/M? The constraints c_k≤M. We can scale: Let t = S^2/M. Maximize t. Equivalent minimize M/S^2. This is a quadratic programming? Variables a and M: maximize t with ∑a=1, c_k≤t/n? Nonconvex.

Could use semidefinite? Not.

Maybe use Fourier: A(z) polynomial. Coefficients of A(z)^2. Want coefficients small. This is like finding polynomial with nonnegative coefficients and small square coefficients. There may be known optimal via trigonometric sums: choose A(z) = (1 - z^n)^? rectangular? Uniform gives coefficients triangular. To reduce peaks, choose A with zero coefficients at edges? A(z) = (1+x+...+x^{m-1}) times something? Products create triangles.

Could use root-of-unity filters. For full (including center), uniform optimal by Cauchy. For off, maybe choose A(z) such that A(z)^2 has one large coefficient at center and rest near lower. If A has positive and negative, can cancel; nonnegative no cancellation. ```

`conv_grad(a,p)`:
```
n=len(a)
L=3*n-2
Lf=1<<(L-1).bit_length()
A=np.fft.rfft(a,Lf)
P=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], Lf)
y=np.fft.irfft(A*P, Lf)
return 2*y[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
```
Need ensure p length 2n-1. If p NaN? no.

```
return e/s
```

`project_simplex(target_sum=1)`: Need efficient for n up to 10000. Use sort O(n log n) per iteration maybe 2000*10000 log ~20M? okay. Implement carefully.
```
def project_simplex(v, target=1.0):
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.nonzero(u * np.arange(1,len(v)+1) > (css[-1]-target))[0]
    if len(rho)==0: return np.maximum(v,0)/max(v.sum(),1e-15)
    r=rho[-1]
    theta=(css[r]-target)/(r+1)
    out=np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
    s=out.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.ones(len(v))*target/len(v)
    return out/(s/target) # sum target
```

```
def active_polish(a,n,sel,factor,lb_score,max_iters,time_limit):
  a=normalize; best=exact_ratio(...); best_a=a
  topk=5
  for it in range(max_iters):
    if time>time_limit: break
    c,S,M=...
    f_score=factor*M/(S*S); f_ratio=M/(S*S); lb_ratio=lb_score/factor
    # active p topk
    vals=c[sel]
    k=min(topk, vals.size)
    idx=np.argpartition(vals,-k)[-k:]
    active=sel[idx]
    p=np.zeros(len(c)); p[active]=1/len(active)
    grad_M=conv_grad_quadratic(a,p) # factor2
    grad=grad_M/(S*S) - 2.0*M/(S**3)*np.ones(n)
    grad -= grad.mean()? not necessary
    norm2=np.dot(grad,grad)
    if norm2 < 1e-30: break
    eta=min(1.0, (f_ratio-lb_ratio)/norm2)
    # If f_ratio<=lb_ratio + tiny, stop.
    improved=False
    for q in range(8):
      a2=a-eta*grad
      a2=np.clip(a2,0,1000)
      s2=a2.sum()
      if s2>1e-15: a2/=s2
      f2=exact_ratio(a2,sel,factor)
      if f2 < best: update best
      if f2 <= f_score*(1+1e-10) or f2 < f_score:
         a=a2; f_score=f2; improved=True; break
      eta*=0.5
    if not improved:
      # perturb a slightly and maybe continue 3 times
      a=perturb... normalize; if no further break
  return best, best_a
```
Use `exact_ratio` recomputes conv each backtracking; expensive. Backtrack 8*conv. Could use current c for a2? a2 changed, need recompute. If iterations 1000, too much. Limit 200. Use no backtrack? Maybe just take step and update if best improves; if not reduce eta and break after few. Keep max_iters 300.

Now correctness of gradient formulas and LSE.

Direct self `z_objective_self` details:
```
def z_objective_self(z, n, beta, sel):
   a=softmax(z)
   c=fast_conv_self(a)
   c_s=c[sel]
   cmax=float(np.max(c_s))
   scaled=beta*(c_s-cmax)
   exp=np.exp(scaled)
   Z=sumexp
   L=cmax + log(Z)/beta
   p_s=exp/Z
   p=np.zeros(2*n-1, dtype=float)
   p[sel]=p_s
   # gradient of L w.r.t a: 2*conv(a, p_rev)[n-1:2n-1][::-1]
   M=3*n-2; Mf=next_pow2(M)
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,Mf)
   P=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1],Mf)
   y=np.fft.irfft(A*P,Mf)
   grad_a=2.0*y[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
   dot=np.dot(a,grad_a)
   grad_z=a*grad_a - a*dot
   # value = L maybe times beta? no
   if not finite: ...
   return float(L), grad_z
```
Disabled factor. But L is LSE of selected c, not max. At high beta approx max. For beta low, approximates (1/beta)logsum, objective large? It may not relate to max but continuation okay. The gradient of LSE over c selected is correct.

One nuance: LSE over selected c (not all) with p normalized. If selected lags count large and beta low, gradient uses p weights. Good.

```
def z_objective_cross(z, f, f_fft, n, beta, sel):
   h=softmax(z)
   Lf=len(f_fft)*2-1? Actually f_fft length (Lf+1)//2, Lf = 3n-2.
   H=np.fft.rfft(h,Lf)
   q=np.fft.irfft(f_fft*H,Lf)[:2*n-1]
   q_s=q[sel]
   cmax=...
   p...
   P=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1],Lf)
   w=np.fft.irfft(f_fft*P,Lf)[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
   dot=np.dot(h,w); grad_z=h*w - h*dot
   return L, grad_z
```
Here `w=F^T p` no factor 2. Check: For f uniform, h uniform, p uniform selected, w? okay.

Need `f_fft` computed with `Lf = next_pow2(3*n-2)`. `np.fft.rfft(f, Lf)` returns length Lf//2+1. Good.

Define `get_sel(n, mode)`:
```
def get_sel(n, mode):
  key=(n,mode)
  if key in _SEL_CACHE: return _SEL_CACHE[key]
  m=2*n-1
  full=np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64)
  if mode==0:
    sel=full
  elif mode==1:
    sel=full[full!=n-1]
  elif mode==2:
    lo=max(0,n-2); hi=min(m-1,n)
    sel=full[(full<lo)|(full>hi)]
  elif mode==3: # exclude 5 around center
    lo=max(0,n-3); hi=min(m-1,n+2)
    sel=full[(full<lo)|(full>hi)]
  else: sel=full
  _SEL_CACHE[key]=sel
  return sel
```
Mode3 maybe unused.

```
return a/s
```

```
def run_lbfgs_self(a0,n,mode,beta_schedule,time_limit,maxiters=None):
   a=normalize_nonneg(a0)
   sel=get_sel(n,mode)
   best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=exact_score(a,sel,2.0*n)
   z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-12))
   if maxiters is None: maxiters=800 if n<=4096 else 400 if n<=8192 else 250
   for beta in beta_schedule:
      if time.monotonic()>time_limit: break
      mi=maxiters if beta<200 else maxiters//2
      try:
       res=minimize(z_objective_self, z, args=(n,beta,sel), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options={'maxiter':mi,'maxfun':min(3*mi,3000),'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8,'maxls':30})
       z=np.asarray(res.x,...)
       a=softmax(z)
       sc=exact_score(a,sel,2.0*n)
       if sc<best_sc: update
       print...
      except Exception: print
   return best_sc,best_a
```

```
def run_cross_improve(f0,n,mode,beta_schedule,time_limit,maxiters=None):

 z=np.zeros(n) # uniform h

   if time>... break
   Lf=_next_pow2(3*n-2); f_fft=np.fft.rfft(f,Lf)
   res=minimize(z_objective_cross, z, args=(f,f_fft,n,beta,sel), ...)
   z=res.x; h=softmax(z)
   # candidate h, blends
   for t in [1.0,0.8,0.6,0.4,0.2,0.1]:
      cand=(1-t)*f+t*h; normalize; sc=exact; update
   f=best; recompute f_fft next? For next beta, use current best f. But cross problem should use current f. Good.
   # if h better than all blends, f=best.
 return best_sc,best_a
```

Now active polish:
```
def active_polish(a0,n,mode,lb_score,time_limit,max_iters=300):
   sel=get_sel; factor=2n
   a=normalize; best=exact; best_a=a
   top_lags=min(5, max(1, n//2000))
   for it in range(max_iters):
     if time>... break
     c=fast_conv_self(a); S=sum; M=max(c[sel]); score=factor*M/S/S
     if score<=lb_score+1e-6: break? but lb_score in score units.
     vals=c[sel]; k=min(top_lags, vals.size); idx=np.argpartition(vals, -k)[-k:]; active=sel[idx]
     p=np.zeros(len(c)); p[active]=1.0/len(active)
     gM=quadratic_grad(a,p)
     g=gM/(S*S) - (2.0*M/(S**3))*np.ones(n)
     # remove mean? Since normalize after, not necessary; but reduce radial: g = g - np.mean(g)
     g = g - np.mean(g)
     norm2=np.dot(g,g)+1e-30
     ratio=M/(S*S); lb_ratio=lb_score/factor
     eta=min(1.0, max(0.0, (ratio-lb_ratio)/norm2))
     if eta <=0: break
     improved=False
     for q in range(6):
       a2=a - eta*g
       a2=np.clip(a2,0,1000); s2=a2.sum()
       if s2>1e-15: a2/=s2
       sc2=exact_score(a2,sel,factor)
       if sc2<best: update
       if sc2 <= score + 1e-12: # allow no grosse increase
          a=a2; score=sc2; improved=True; break
       eta*=0.5
     if not improved:
       # small perturbation: noise to a
       a = normalize(a + 0.01/rng? but no rng param)
```
Need rng for polish; pass rng. If no improvement several times break. Use `stall` counter.
`lb_score` for off maybe 1.42; if score already <lb+? stop. For full use 1.95? But active may run full. We'll call with mode off lb=1.42, mode full lb=1.95? If score below 2 okay.
If internal score <lb due approximate? no.

Potential issue: `g - mean(g)` after ratio gradient may not be correct but helps.

`gaussian_perturb(a, rng, sigma)`:


`targeted_perturb(a, mode, rng, strength)`: Use sel; active top 1 or 3; contrib=0.5*grad with p one active. Move mass from top N to bottom N.
```
def targeted_perturb(a, mode, rng, strength):
   n=len(a); sel=get_sel; c=fast; vals=c[sel]; k=1; idx=argmax; active=sel[idx]
   p=zeros; p[active]=1; contrib=0.5*qgrad(a,p)
   top_count=max(3, n//200)
   order=np.argsort(contrib)[::-1]
   top=order[:top_count]
   bottom=order[top_count:]
   if len(bottom)<top_count: bottom=order[:top_count]
   a2=a.copy(); moved=0
   for j in top: amt=a2[j]*strength; a2[j]-=amt; a2[j]=max(0); moved+=amt
   if moved>0 and len(bottom)>0: per=moved/len(bottom); for j in bottom: a2[j]+=per
   normalize
```
Use top_count maybe n//100.

Detailed functions:

```
def _fft_conv(u, v):
    u=np.asarray(u,float); v=np.asarray(v,float)
    m=len(u)+len(v)-1
    mfft=1 << (m-1).bit_length()
    U=np.fft.rfft(u, mfft)
    V=np.fft.rfft(v, mfft)
    return np.fft.irfft(U*V, mfft)[:m]
```
`fast_conv_self` use separate maybe more efficient:
```
def fast_conv_self(a):
   n=len(a); m=2*n-1; mfft=1 << (m-1).bit_length()
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,mfft); c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,mfft)[:m]
   return c
```

`quadratic_grad(a,p)`:
```
def quadratic_grad(a,p):
    n=len(a); L=3*n-2; Lf=1 << (L-1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,Lf); P=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1],Lf)
    y=np.fft.irfft(A*P,Lf)
    return 2.0*y[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
```
Cross gradient no factor 2:
```
def cross_grad(f,p,f_fft=None,Lf=None):
   n=len(f)
   if f_fft is None or Lf is None:
      Lf=1 << ((3*n-2)-1).bit_length(); f_fft=np.fft.rfft(f,Lf)
   P=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1],Lf)
   y=np.fft.irfft(f_fft*P,Lf)
   return y[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
```
But if f_fft length matches. Good.

`softmax`:
```
def softmax(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,float).ravel()
    if z.size==0: return np.array([])
    z=np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    zmax=float(np.max(z))
    e=np.exp(z-zmax)
    s=e.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0:
       return np.ones(z.size)/z.size
    return e/s
```
If z contains large range >700, exp of z-zmax safe (≤0). Good.

`normalize_nonneg`:
```
def normalize_nonneg(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
   s=float(a.sum())
   n=len(a)
   if n==0: return np.ones(1)
   if s<=1e-15: return np.ones(n)/n
   return a/s
```

`to_list`:
```
def to_list(a, target_sum=1.0):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   n=len(a)
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0); a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<=1e-15:
      return [float(target_sum/n)]*n if n>0 else [1.0]
   b=a*(target_sum/s)
   mx=float(b.max())
   if mx>900.0:
      b=b*(900.0/mx)
   s=b.sum()
   if s<0.01:
      # add tiny uniform
      add=(0.01 - s)/n
      b=b+max(add,1e-12)
      if b.max()>900: b=np.minimum(b,900)
   b=np.clip(b,0.0,1000.0)
   return [float(x) for x in b]
```
If target_sum=1 and b max<1000. Good.

`get_sel` as above.

```
def conv_and_score(a, sel, factor):
```

Objective self:
```
def z_objective_self(z, n, beta, sel):
    try:
       a=softmax(z)
       c=fast_conv_self(a)
       cs=c[sel]
       if cs.size==0: return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
       cmax=float(np.max(cs))
       if cmax <=0: cmax=0.0
       scaled=float(beta)*(cs-cmax)
       expv=np.exp(scaled)
       zsum=float(expv.sum())
       if zsum<=0 or not np.isfinite(zsum): return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
       L=cmax + np.log(zsum)/float(beta)
       ps=expv/zsum
       p=np.zeros(c.shape, dtype=float)
       p[sel]=ps
       Lf=1 << ((3*n-2)-1).bit_length()
       A=np.fft.rfft(a,Lf)
       P=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1],Lf)
       y=np.fft.irfft(A*P,Lf)
       g=a* (2.0*y[n-1:2*n-1][::-1])
       dot=float(np.dot(a,g))
```

```
def maxiter_for(n, idx, total):
  base=900 if n<=4096 else 450 if n<=8192 else 280
  frac=[1.0,1.0,0.7,0.5,0.35]
  mi=int(base*frac[min(idx,4)])
  return max(80,mi)
```

```
def legendre_seq(m):
   m=int(m)
   if m % 2 == 0: m += 1
   if m < 3: m=3
   x=np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64)
   s=np.ones(m)
   s[(x*x) % m != 1] = -1
   # maybe improve: s[x == 0]=0? We can set 0? For nonnegative a, zeros okay but softmax init log eps. Set 0? use 1.
   return s
```

Build inits details:
```
def build_inits(n, rng, parent):
  inits=[]
  if parent is not None:
     if parent.size == n: p=parent
     elif parent.size > 0 and 512 <= n <= 20000:
        xp=np.linspace(0.0,parent.size-1.0,parent.size)
        x=np.linspace(0.0,n-1.0,n)
        p=np.interp(x, xp, parent)
     else: p=None
     if p is not None:
        p=normalize_nonneg(p); inits.append(p)
  # uniform
  inits.append(np.ones(n)/n)
  x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
  # smooth random add
  for gamma in [0.1,0.3,0.5,0.8]:
     noise=rng.standard_normal(n)
     # lowpass via moving average
     w=max(3, n//50)
     # simple cumulative smoothing: use np.convolve? Could be O(nw). w=140, n=7000 ->1M ok.
     kernel=np.ones(w); kernel/=w
     noise=np.convolve(noise,kernel,'same')
     a=normalize_nonneg(np.clip(1.0+gamma*noise,0.0,10.0))
     inits.append(a)
     if len(inits)>=12: break
  # cosines
  for _ in range(3):
     a=np.ones(n)
     for _ in range(2):
        freq=1.0+8.0*rng.random()
        amp=0.05+0.4*rng.random()
        ph=2*np.pi*rng.random()
        a += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*freq*x+ph)
     inits.append(normalize_nonneg(np.clip(a,0.01,10.0)))
  # tapered
  inits.append(normalize_nonneg(1.0 + 1.5*np.sin(np.pi*x)**2))
  inits.append(normalize_nonneg(np.sin(np.pi*x)**2 + 0.1))
  # Legendre
  m = max(3, n if n%2==1 else n-1)
  # Maybe too long; use m=n or n//2? Use n.
  s=legendre_seq(m)
  if len(s)<n: s=np.tile(s, (n//len(s))+1)[:n]
  else: s=s[:n]
  for eps in [0.05,0.15,0.35]:
     inits.append(normalize_nonneg(1.0 + eps*s))
     inits.append(normalize_nonneg(np.square(1.0 + eps*s)))
  # alternating
  alt = np.where(np.arange(n)%2==0,1.0,-1.0)
  env = 0.5 + 0.5*np.sin(np.pi*x)**2
  inits.append(normalize_nonneg(1.0 + 0.2*alt*env + 0.1*alt))
  inits.append(normalize_nonneg(np.power(1.0 + 0.25*alt, 2)))
  # deduplicate? Limit 12
  return inits[:12]
```

```
def mirror_cross_min(f,n,sel,iters=600,beta=200,time_limit=None):
   Lf=...; f_fft=np.fft.rfft(f,Lf)
   log_h = np.zeros(n)
   log_p = np.zeros(sel.size)
   best_h = None; best_val = inf
   eta = 10.0 * n / iters  # ~117 for n=7000,iters600
   # this might be large; cumulative average reduces.
   for t in range(iters):
      h=softmax(log_h)
      H=rfft(h,Lf); q=irfft(f_fft*H,Lf)[:2n-1]; qs=q[sel]
      qmax=qs.max(); p_log = beta*(qs-qmax)
      # smooth p_log: log_p = 0.7*log_p + 0.3*p_log? log_p cumulative?
      log_p = 0.6*log_p + 0.4*p_log
      P=softmax(log_p)
      # build p full? cross_grad needs p full. We can avoid building full every iter by computing w via convolution of f with p_rev where p full zeros except sel. Need allocate p full. length 14000, okay.
      p_full=np.zeros(2n-1); p_full[sel]=P
      w=cross_grad(f,p_full,f_fft,Lf)
      # update log_h += -eta*(w - w.min())
      log_h += -eta * (w - float(np.min(w)))
      # periodically softmax stable; also renormalize log_h mean 0 to prevent drift
      if t % 50 == 0:
         h_avg=softmax(log_h)
         q=conv... compute m; if m<best_val: best_h=h_avg
   return softmax(log_h), best_h if best_score better
```

```
def load_parent():

      obj=None

      if p.size>0 and p.sum()>1e-12: return p
```

`eval_safe` top-level:
```
def eval_safe(lst):
   try:
      val=float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
      if not np.isfinite(val): return np.inf
      return val
   except Exception:
```

`targeted_perturb`:
```
def targeted_perturb(a, mode, rng, strength):
    n=len(a)
    sel=get_sel(n,mode)
    if sel.size==0: return a.copy()
    c=fast_conv_self(a)
    vals=c[sel]
    idx=int(np.argmax(vals))
    k=sel[idx]
    p=np.zeros(2*n-1)
    p[k]=1.0
    contrib=0.5*quadratic_grad(a,p)
    contrib=np.where(np.isfinite(contrib),contrib,0.0)
    top_count=max(3, min(n//100, n//2))
    if top_count >= n: return a.copy()
    order=np.argsort(contrib)[::-1]
    top=order[:top_count]
    bottom=order[top_count:n-top_count] if n-2*top_count>0 else order[top_count:]
    a2=a.copy()
    moved=0.0
    for j in top:
       amt=a2[j]*strength
       a2[j]-=amt
       if a2[j]<0: a2[j]=0
       moved+=a2[j]? Wait if clamp, moved should actual reduced = amt - max(0)-? Simpler:
       old=a2[j]; new=max(0, old-amt); moved += old-new; a2[j]=new
    if len(bottom)>0 and moved>0:
       per=moved/len(bottom)
       for j in bottom: a2[j]=min(1000.0, a2[j]+per)
    return normalize_nonneg(a2)
```
If bottom empty, use all indices.

`gaussian_perturb`:
```
def gaussian_perturb(a, rng, sigma):
   z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-12))
   z = z + sigma*rng.standard_normal(len(a))
   z = z - np.mean(z)
   return softmax(z)
```
`sinusoidal_perturb`:
```
def sinusoidal_perturb(a, rng):
   n=len(a); x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   freq=1+8*rng.random(); ph=2*np.pi*rng.random(); amp=0.05+0.25*rng.random()
   mult=1+amp*np.cos(2πfreqx+ph)
   return normalize_nonneg(np.maximum(a*mult,0))
```

Let's update all gradient functions:
```
def quad_grad(a,p):
   n=len(a)
   conv=fft_conv(a[::-1], p)
   return 2.0*conv[n-1:2*n-1]
def cross_grad(f,p):
   n=len(f)
   conv=fft_conv(f[::-1], p)
   return conv[n-1:2*n-1]
```

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative sequences minimizing the (possibly black-box) evaluation
score.  The program runs several complementary routines:

  1. Multi-start smoothed LSE-Self optimization over the simplex, using
     softmax parametrization.  It is run for several possible lag sets
     (full, off-center, wider off-center) so that the program remains useful
     if the hidden active lag set is not exactly one of these.

  2. Cross-convolution minimization: given f, minimize max_k (f*h)_k over
     h in the simplex.  This is a convex approximation related to the LP
     state-of-the-art; the resulting h and blends with f are optimized.

  3. Exact active-set projected subgradient polish on the exact max-conv score.

  4. Black-box refinement using evaluate_sequence() directly, including
     random logit perturbations and peak-targeted perturbations.

All heavy numeric work uses FFT-based convolution and softmax-represented
simplex variables.  The returned list is the best sequence found under the
actual evaluate_sequence() call.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_SEL_CACHE = {}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Basic helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _next_pow2(m: int) -> int:
    """Smallest power of two >= m."""
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (int(m) - 1).bit_length()


def _fft_conv(u, v):
    """Real linear convolution by FFT."""
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    m = len(u) + len(v) - 1
    mf = _next_pow2(m)
    U = np.fft.rfft(u, mf)
    V = np.fft.rfft(v, mf)
    return np.fft.irfft(U * V, mf)[:m]


def fast_conv_self(a):
    """Linear self-convolution of a with a."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    mf = _next_pow2(m)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, mf)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, mf)[:m]
    return c


def quadratic_grad(a, p):
    """
    Gradient of sum_k p_k c_k where c = conv(a,a).

    For c_k = sum_i a_i a_{k-i},
    d/d a_j = 2 sum_k p_k a_{k-j}  where 0 <= k-j < n.

    This is 2 * (rev(a) conv p)[n-1:2n-1].
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    conv = _fft_conv(a[::-1], p)
    return 2.0 * conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def cross_grad(f, p):
    """
    Gradient (linear) operator F^T p for q = conv(f, h).

    w_j = sum_k p_k f_{k-j} where 0 <= k-j < n.
    This is (rev(f) conv p)[n-1:2n-1].
    """
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(f)
    conv = _fft_conv(f[::-1], p)
    return conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def softmax_keep_positive(z):
    """Softmax with numerical safety."""
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = z.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    zmax = float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z - zmax)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return e / s


def normalize_nonneg(a):
    """Make a nonnegative finite vector summing to 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return a / s


def to_output_list(a, target_sum=1.0):
    """Convert internal array to a valid Python list for evaluate_sequence."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return [1.0]
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        val = float(target_sum) / float(n)
        if not np.isfinite(val) or val <= 0.0:
            val = 1.0 / float(n)
        return [float(val)] * n

    b = a * (float(target_sum) / s)
    b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
    mx = float(b.max())
    if mx > 900.0:
        b = b * (900.0 / mx)

    s2 = float(b.sum())
    if s2 < 0.01:
        add = max(1e-12, (0.01 - s2) / float(n))
        b = b + add
        if float(b.max()) > 900.0:
            b = np.minimum(b, 900.0)

    b = np.clip(b, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return [float(x) for x in b]


def get_selected_indices(n, mode):
    """
    mode 0: all lags
    mode 1: all lags except exact center n-1
    mode 2: all lags except {n-2, n-1, n}
    mode 3: all lags except a wider center window
    """
    key = (int(n), int(mode))
    if key in _SEL_CACHE:
        return _SEL_CACHE[key]

    m = 2 * n - 1
    full = np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64)
    if mode == 0:
        sel = full
    elif mode == 1:
        sel = full[full != (n - 1)]
    elif mode == 2:
        lo = max(0, n - 2)
        hi = min(m - 1, n)
        sel = full[(full < lo) | (full > hi)]
    elif mode == 3:
        lo = max(0, n - 3)
        hi = min(m - 1, n + 2)
        sel = full[(full < lo) | (full > hi)]
    else:
        sel = full

    _SEL_CACHE[key] = sel
    return sel


def exact_score_and_conv(a, sel, factor):
    """Return (convolution, score).  Score = factor * max_sel / sum^2."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    c = fast_conv_self(a)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return c, np.inf
    if sel.size == 0:
        M = 0.0
    else:
        M = float(np.max(c[sel]))
        if M < 0.0 and M > -1e-12:
            M = 0.0
    score = float(factor * M / (s * s))
    return c, score


def exact_score(a, sel, factor):
    _, score = exact_score_and_conv(a, sel, factor)
    return score


def eval_safe(lst):
    """Call global evaluate_sequence safely."""
    try:
        val = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
    except Exception:
        return np.inf
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return np.inf
    return val


def load_parent():
    """Load the optional height_sequence_1 construction."""
    obj = None
    try:
        obj = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    except Exception:
        obj = None

    if obj is None:
        return None

    try:
        p = np.asarray(obj, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if p.size >= 8 and p.sum() > 1e-12:
            return p
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def add_candidate(cand_list, a, score):
    """Append a normalized candidate to a list, keeping it bounded."""
    if a is None:
        return
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    if len(cand_list) > 80:
        cand_list.pop(0)
    cand_list.append((float(score), a.copy()))


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Smooth LSE objectives for scipy
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def z_objective_self(z, n, beta, sel):
    """
    Softmax parametrization of a self-convolution LSE objective.

    The selected convolution coefficients are scaled by n so that their
    maximum is O(1) for probability-vector inputs.  The returned gradient is
    for the logit variable z.
    """
    try:
        a = softmax_keep_positive(z)
        c = fast_conv_self(a)
        if sel.size == 0:
            return 1e100, np.zeros(len(z), dtype=np.float64)

        cs = c[sel] * float(n)
        cs = np.where(np.isfinite(cs), cs, 0.0)
        cmax = float(np.max(cs))
        if cmax <= 0.0:
            cmax = 0.0

        scaled = float(beta) * (cs - cmax)
        expv = np.exp(scaled)
        zsum = float(expv.sum())
        if zsum <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(zsum):
            return 1e100, np.zeros(len(z), dtype=np.float64)

        L = cmax + float(np.log(zsum)) / float(beta)
        ps = expv / zsum

        p = np.zeros(c.shape, dtype=np.float64)
        p[sel] = ps

        grad_a = float(n) * quadratic_grad(a, p)
        grad_a = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_a), grad_a, 0.0)
        dot = float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
        grad_z = a * (grad_a - dot)
        grad_z = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_z), grad_z, 0.0)

        if not np.isfinite(L):
            L = 1e100
        return float(L), grad_z
    except Exception:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(len(z), dtype=np.float64)


def z_objective_cross(z, f, f_fft, f_rev_fft, Lf, n, beta, sel):
    """
    Softmax parametrization of cross-convolution LSE objective:
        h in simplex, minimize LSE_max( (f*h)_sel ).
    """
    try:
        h = softmax_keep_positive(z)
        H = np.fft.rfft(h, Lf)
        q = np.fft.irfft(f_fft * H, Lf)[:2 * n - 1]

        if sel.size == 0:
            return 1e100, np.zeros(len(z), dtype=np.float64)

        qs = q[sel] * float(n)
        qs = np.where(np.isfinite(qs), qs, 0.0)
        cmax = float(np.max(qs))
        if cmax <= 0.0:
            cmax = 0.0

        scaled = float(beta) * (qs - cmax)
        expv = np.exp(scaled)
        zsum = float(expv.sum())
        if zsum <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(zsum):
            return 1e100, np.zeros(len(z), dtype=np.float64)

        L = cmax + float(np.log(zsum)) / float(beta)
        ps = expv / zsum

        p = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        p[sel] = ps

        P = np.fft.rfft(p, Lf)
        w = np.fft.irfft(f_rev_fft * P, Lf)[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
        w = float(n) * w
        w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)

        dot = float(np.dot(h, w))
        grad_z = h * (w - dot)
        grad_z = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_z), grad_z, 0.0)

        if not np.isfinite(L):
            L = 1e100
        return float(L), grad_z
    except Exception:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(len(z), dtype=np.float64)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# L-BFGS drivers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def maxiter_base_for(n):
    """Default L-BFGS maxiter base by problem size."""
    if n <= 4096:
        return 900
    if n <= 8192:
        return 450
    if n <= 12000:
        return 320
    return 220


def run_lbfgs_self(a0, n, mode, beta_schedule, time_limit, maxiter_base=None):
    """Continuation LSE self-convolution minimization under a normalized sum."""
    a0 = normalize_nonneg(a0)
    sel = get_selected_indices(n, mode)
    factor = 2.0 * float(n)

    if sel.size == 0:
        return np.inf, a0

    best_a = a0.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score(a0, sel, factor)

    z = np.log(np.maximum(a0, 1e-12))
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))

    if maxiter_base is None:
        base = maxiter_base_for(n)
    else:
        base = int(maxiter_base)

    fracs = [1.0, 1.0, 0.75, 0.6, 0.45, 0.35]

    for bi, beta in enumerate(beta_schedule):
        if time.monotonic() > time_limit:
            break
        mi = int(base * fracs[min(bi, len(fracs) - 1)])
        mi = max(60, mi)
        mf = max(2 * mi, 300)

        try:
            res = minimize(
                z_objective_self,
                z,
                args=(n, int(beta), sel),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                options={
                    "ftol": 1e-13,
                    "gtol": 1e-7,
                    "maxiter": mi,
                    "maxfun": mf,
                    "maxls": 30,
                },
            )
            new_z = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if new_z.size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(new_z)):
                new_z = new_z - float(np.mean(new_z))
                a_new = softmax_keep_positive(new_z)
                sc_new = exact_score(a_new, sel, factor)
                if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_sc:
                    best_sc = float(sc_new)
                    best_a = a_new.copy()
                z = new_z
            # print moderate logging only
            print(f"  self n={n} mode={mode} beta={beta} best={best_sc:.8f}")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"  self lbfgs error n={n} beta={beta}: {e}")

    sc_final = exact_score(best_a, sel, factor)
    if np.isfinite(sc_final) and sc_final < best_sc:
        best_sc = float(sc_final)
    return float(best_sc), best_a


def run_cross_improve(f0, n, mode, beta_schedule, time_limit, maxiter_base=None):
    """Alternating cross-minimization step: improve f by minimizing f*h."""
    f = normalize_nonneg(f0)
    sel = get_selected_indices(n, mode)
    factor = 2.0 * float(n)

    if sel.size == 0:
        return np.inf, f

    best_f = f.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score(f, sel, factor)

    z_h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    if maxiter_base is None:
        base = maxiter_base_for(n)
    else:
        base = int(maxiter_base)

    fracs = [1.0, 0.85, 0.7, 0.55, 0.45]

    for bi, beta in enumerate(beta_schedule):
        if time.monotonic() > time_limit:
            break

        try:
            Lf = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
            f_fft = np.fft.rfft(f, Lf)
            f_rev_fft = np.fft.rfft(f[::-1], Lf)

            mi = int(base * fracs[min(bi, len(fracs) - 1)])
            mi = max(60, mi)
            mf = max(2 * mi, 300)

            res = minimize(
                z_objective_cross,
                z_h,
                args=(f, f_fft, f_rev_fft, Lf, n, int(beta), sel),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                options={
                    "ftol": 1e-13,
                    "gtol": 1e-7,
                    "maxiter": mi,
                    "maxfun": mf,
                    "maxls": 30,
                },
            )

            new_z = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if new_z.size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(new_z)):
                new_z = new_z - float(np.mean(new_z))
                h = softmax_keep_positive(new_z)
                z_h = new_z
            else:
                h = softmax_keep_positive(z_h)

            # Try h itself and blends with current f.
            for t in (1.0, 0.85, 0.7, 0.55, 0.4, 0.25, 0.1):
                cand = (1.0 - t) * f + t * h
                cand = normalize_nonneg(cand)
                sc = exact_score(cand, sel, factor)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = float(sc)
                    best_f = cand.copy()

            if time.monotonic() < time_limit:
                polish_sc, polish_a = active_polish(
                    best_f, n, mode, 1.40,
                    min(time_limit, time.monotonic() + 45.0),
                    None, 50
                )
                if np.isfinite(polish_sc) and polish_sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = float(polish_sc)
                    best_f = polish_a.copy()

            # Use the improved sequence for the next cross problem.
            f = best_f.copy()
            print(f"  cross n={n} mode={mode} beta={beta} best={best_sc:.8f}")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"  cross error n={n} beta={beta}: {e}")

    sc_final = exact_score(best_f, sel, factor)
    if np.isfinite(sc_final) and sc_final < best_sc:
        best_sc = float(sc_final)
    return float(best_sc), best_f


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Exact active-set polish
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def active_polish(a0, n, mode, lb_score, time_limit, rng, max_iters=200):
    """
    Projected active-set subgradient polish on the exact max score.
    """
    a = normalize_nonneg(a0)
    sel = get_selected_indices(n, mode)
    factor = 2.0 * float(n)

    if sel.size == 0:
        return np.inf, a

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score(a, sel, factor)
    lb_ratio = float(lb_score) / float(factor)
    stall = 0

    for _ in range(int(max_iters)):
        if time.monotonic() > time_limit:
            break

        c, sc = exact_score_and_best_active = exact_score_and_conv(a, sel, factor)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 1e-15:
            break
        M = float(np.max(c[sel])) if sel.size else 0.0
        ratio = M / (s * s)

        if sc <= best_sc and sc <= lb_score + 1e-9:
            break
        if ratio <= lb_ratio + 1e-12:
            break

        vals = c[sel]
        top_lags = max(1, min(4, n // 2000 + 1))
        if vals.size <= top_lags:
            active = sel
        else:
            top = np.argpartition(vals, -top_lags)[-top_lags:]
            active = sel[top]

        p = np.zeros(c.shape, dtype=np.float64)
        p[active] = 1.0 / float(active.size)

        grad_M = quadratic_grad(a, p)
        grad_M = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_M), grad_M, 0.0)

        # Gradient of M/S^2:
        #   d(M/S^2) = grad_M/S^2 - 2M (1)/S^3
        grad = grad_M / (s * s) - (2.0 * M / (s * s * s)) * np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        grad = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
        norm2 = float(np.dot(grad, grad))
        if norm2 <= 1e-30:
            break

        eta = (ratio - lb_ratio) / norm2
        if not np.isfinite(eta) or eta <= 0.0:
            eta = 0.02
        eta = min(1.0, eta)

        improved = False
        for _bt in range(7):
            a2 = a - eta * grad
            a2 = np.clip(a2, 0.0, 1000.0)
            s2 = float(a2.sum())
            if s2 > 1e-15:
                a2 = a2 / s2
            sc2 = exact_score(a2, sel, factor)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                best_sc = float(sc2)
                best_a = a2.copy()
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 <= sc + 1e-11:
                a = a2
                sc = float(sc2)
                improved = True
                break
            eta *= 0.5

        if improved:
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall >= 6:
                break

    sc_final = exact_score(best_a, sel, factor)
    if np.isfinite(sc_final) and sc_final < best_sc:
        best_sc = float(sc_final)
    return float(best_sc), best_a


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Random / targeted perturbations
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def gaussian_perturb(a, rng, sigma):
    z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-12))
    z = z + float(sigma) * rng.standard_normal(len(a))
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    return softmax_keep_positive(z)


def sinusoidal_perturb(a, rng):
    n = len(a)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    freq = 1.0 + 8.0 * rng.random()
    phase = 2.0 * np.pi * rng.random()
    amp = 0.05 + 0.25 * rng.random()
    mult = 1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x + phase)
    return normalize_nonneg(np.maximum(a * mult, 0.0))


def targeted_perturb(a, mode, rng, strength):
    """Reduce components contributing most to the largest selected lag."""
    n = len(a)
    sel = get_selected_indices(n, mode)
    if sel.size == 0:
        return a.copy()

    c = fast_conv_self(a)
    k = int(sel[int(np.argmax(c[sel]))])

    p = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    p[k] = 1.0
    contrib = 0.5 * quadratic_grad(a, p)
    contrib = np.where(np.isfinite(contrib), contrib, 0.0)

    top_count = max(3, min(n // 100, n // 2))
    if top_count >= n:
        return a.copy()

    order = np.argsort(contrib)[::-1]
    top = order[:top_count]
    if n - 2 * top_count > 0:
        bottom = order[top_count:n - top_count]
    else:
        bottom = order[top_count:]

    a2 = a.copy()
    moved = 0.0
    for j in top:
        old = a2[j]
        new = max(0.0, old - strength * old)
        moved += old - new
        a2[j] = new

    if moved > 0.0 and bottom.size > 0:
        per = moved / float(bottom.size)
        for j in bottom:
            v = a2[j] + per
            a2[j] = min(1000.0, max(0.0, v))

    return normalize_nonneg(a2)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Initializations
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def legendre_sequence(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m < 3:
        m = 3
    if m % 2 == 0:
        m += 1
    x = np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64)
    s = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)
    s[(x * x) % m != 1] = -1.0
    return s


def build_inits(n, rng, parent):
    """Generate diverse nonnegative probability initial vectors of length n."""
    inits = []

    if parent is not None:
        try:
            if parent.size == n:
                pparent = parent
            else:
                if parent.size >= 2 and n <= 20000:
                    xp = np.linspace(0.0, parent.size - 1.0, parent.size)
                    xq = np.linspace(0.0, n - 1.0, n)
                    pparent = np.interp(xq, xp, parent)
                else:
                    pparent = None
            if pparent is not None:
                inits.append(normalize_nonneg(pparent))
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Uniform.
    inits.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)

    # Smooth lowpass Gaussian noise.
    for gamma in (0.12, 0.28, 0.5, 0.85):
        noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
        w = max(3, min(151, n // 40))
        ker = np.ones(w, dtype=np.float64)
        ker /= float(w)
        noise = np.convolve(noise, ker, mode="same")
        a = np.clip(1.0 + gamma * noise, 1e-3, 50.0)
        inits.append(normalize_nonneg(a))
        if len(inits) >= 10:
            break

    # Multi-sinusoidal shapes.
    for _ in range(3):
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for _ in range(2):
            freq = 1.0 + 7.0 * rng.random()
            amp = 0.05 + 0.35 * rng.random()
            ph = 2.0 * np.pi * rng.random()
            a += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x + ph)
        inits.append(normalize_nonneg(np.clip(a, 1e-3, 50.0)))

    # Tapered / envelope shapes.
    inits.append(normalize_nonneg(1.0 + 1.8 * np.sin(np.pi * x) ** 2))
    inits.append(normalize_nonneg(0.05 + np.sin(np.pi * x) ** 2))
    inits.append(normalize_nonneg(1.0 + 0.8 * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2 + 0.3))

    # Legendre-style two-level modulation.
    try:
        m = n if n % 2 == 1 else n - 1
        m = max(3, m)
        s = legendre_sequence(m)
        if s.size < n:
            s = np.tile(s, (n // s.size) + 1)[:n]
        else:
            s = s[:n]
        for eps in (0.06, 0.18, 0.36):
            inits.append(normalize_nonneg(np.clip(1.0 + eps * s, 1e-3, 50.0)))
            inits.append(normalize_nonneg(np.square(1.0 + eps * s)))
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Alternating envelope.
    alt = np.where(np.arange(n) % 2 == 0, 1.0, -1.0)
    env = 0.3 + 0.7 * np.sin(np.pi * x) ** 2
    inits.append(normalize_nonneg(1.0 + 0.18 * alt * env + 0.08 * alt))
    inits.append(normalize_nonneg(np.square(1.0 + 0.22 * alt)))

    # Random block/tile shape.
    try:
        nb = max(8, min(64, n // 64 + 8))
        per = max(1, n // nb)
        hv = 0.35 + 0.65 * rng.random(nb)
        arr = np.repeat(hv, per)
        if arr.size < n:
            arr = np.concatenate([arr, hv[:n - arr.size]])
        a = np.clip(arr[:n], 1e-3, 1e3)
        inits.append(normalize_nonneg(a))
    except Exception:
        pass

    return inits[:16]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Black-box local refinement
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def blackbox_refine(a0, n, rng, time_limit, best_holder):
    """Random/targeted black-box refinement using actual evaluate_sequence()."""
    if n > 16000:
        return

    a = normalize_nonneg(a0)
    cur_score = float(best_holder[0])
    cur_list = best_holder[1]

    # Evaluate current as a list with sum 1.
    lst0 = to_output_list(a, 1.0)
    v0 = eval_safe(lst0)
    if np.isfinite(v0) and v0 < cur_score:
        cur_score = float(v0)
        cur_list = lst0
        best_holder[0] = cur_score
        best_holder[1] = lst0

    max_trials = 250
    for trial in range(max_trials):
        if time.monotonic() > time_limit:
            break

        strat = trial % 5
        if strat == 0:
            sigma = 0.04 + 0.18 * rng.random()
            a2 = gaussian_perturb(a, rng, sigma)
        elif strat == 1:
            a2 = gaussian_perturb(a, rng, 0.25 + 0.35 * rng.random())
        elif strat == 2:
            a2 = targeted_perturb(a, 1, rng, 0.04 + 0.12 * rng.random())
        elif strat == 3:
            a2 = targeted_perturb(a, 0, rng, 0.03 + 0.10 * rng.random())
        else:
            a2 = sinusoidal_perturb(a, rng)

        a2 = normalize_nonneg(a2)
        lst2 = to_output_list(a2, 1.0)
        v2 = eval_safe(lst2)
        if np.isfinite(v2) and v2 < cur_score:
            cur_score = float(v2)
            cur_list = lst2
            best_holder[0] = cur_score
            best_holder[1] = lst2
            a = a2

        # Occasionally run a short internal LSE polish from the best point.
        if trial % 6 == 0 and time.monotonic() < time_limit - 30.0:
            try:
                sc3, a3 = run_lbfgs_self(
                    a, n, 1, [800],
                    time_limit, maxiter_base=110
                )
                if np.isfinite(sc3):
                    lst3 = to_output_list(a3, 1.0)
                    v3 = eval_safe(lst3)
                    if np.isfinite(v3) and v3 < cur_score:
                        cur_score = float(v3)
                        cur_list = lst3
                        best_holder[0] = cur_score
                        best_holder[1] = lst3
                        a = normalize_nonneg(a3)
            except Exception:
                pass

        if trial % 50 == 0 and trial > 0:
            print(f"  bb trial={trial} best_actual={cur_score:.8f} at t={time.monotonic():.0f}s")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main entry point
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Search candidate and return the best list found within budget_s seconds.
    Assumes evaluate_sequence() is already in the global namespace.
    """
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t_start + total - 25.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    parent = load_parent()
    candidates = []

    # Baseline safe candidate.
    base = np.ones(2048, dtype=np.float64) / 2048.0
    add_candidate(candidates, base, 2.0)

    if parent is not None:
        add_candidate(candidates, parent, np.nan)

    # Build length schedule.
    lengths = []
    if parent is not None:
        lp = int(parent.size)
        if 512 <= lp <= 12000 and lp not in lengths:
            lengths.append(lp)
    for t in (4096, 6962, 8192):
        if t not in lengths:
            lengths.append(t)
    lengths = sorted(set(lengths))
    if len(lengths) > 3:
        lengths = lengths[:3]

    print(f"[start] lengths={lengths}, parent={'yes' if parent is not None else 'no'}")

    phase_a_end = t_start + 0.52 * total
    phase_b_end = t_start + 0.78 * total
    phase_c_end = t_start + 0.93 * total

    main_modes = [1, 2]

    for n in lengths:
        if time.monotonic() > phase_a_end:
            break

        print(f"\n=== length n={n} ===")
        inits = build_inits(n, rng, parent)
        if not inits:
            inits = [np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)]

        local_best_a = inits[0]
        local_best_sc = exact_score(local_best_a, get_selected_indices(n, 1), 2.0 * float(n))

        # Direct self-convolution optimization, mode 1 first.
        for ii, init in enumerate(inits[:6]):
            if time.monotonic() > phase_a_end:
                break

            init = normalize_nonneg(init)
            if ii == 0:
                betas = [30, 100, 300, 800, 1500] if n <= 8192 else [40, 120, 400, 1000]
                mb = None
            else:
                betas = [60, 200, 600]
                mb = 260 if n <= 5000 else 170

            sc, a_out = run_lbfgs_self(init, n, 1, betas, phase_a_end, maxiter_base=mb)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                add_candidate(candidates, a_out, sc)
                if sc < local_best_sc:
                    local_best_sc = float(sc)
                    local_best_a = a_out.copy()

            # Cross improvement from the first two direct results.
            if ii < 2 and time.monotonic() < phase_a_end - 60.0:
                try:
                    scx, ax = run_cross_improve(
                        a_out, n, 1, [50, 200, 600],
                        phase_a_end - 60.0, maxiter_base=200
                    )
                    if np.isfinite(scx):
                        add_candidate(candidates, ax, scx)
                        if scx < local_best_sc:
                            local_best_sc = float(scx)
                            local_best_a = ax.copy()
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"  cross improvement failed for n={n}, init={ii}: {e}")

            # Short active polish.
            if time.monotonic() < phase_a_end - 30.0:
                try:
                    scp, ap = active_polish(
                        a_out, n, 1, 1.40,
                        phase_a_end - 30.0, rng, 90
                    )
                    if np.isfinite(scp):
                        add_candidate(candidates, ap, scp)
                        if scp < local_best_sc:
                            local_best_sc = float(scp)
                            local_best_a = ap.copy()
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"  active polish failed for n={n}, init={ii}: {e}")

        # If time remains, do a slightly stronger cross improvement locally.
        if local_best_a is not None and time.monotonic() < phase_a_end - 20.0:
            try:
                scx, ax = run_cross_improve(
                    local_best_a, n, 1, [100, 400],
                    phase_a_end - 20.0, maxiter_base=220
                )
                if np.isfinite(scx):
                    add_candidate(candidates, ax, scx)
                    if scx < local_best_sc:
                        local_best_sc = float(scx)
                        local_best_a = ax.copy()
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"  final cross failed n={n}: {e}")

        # Mode 2: exclude a wider center window.
        if local_best_a is not None and time.monotonic() < phase_b_end:
            try:
                sc2, a2 = run_lbfgs_self(
                    local_best_a, n, 2,
                    [80, 300, 800],
                    phase_b_end,
                    maxiter_base=180
                )
                if np.isfinite(sc2):
                    add_candidate(candidates, a2, sc2)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"  mode2 self failed n={n}: {e}")

            if time.monotonic() < phase_b_end:
                try:
                    sc2p, a2p = active_polish(
                        a2 if 'a2' in locals() and a2 is not None else local_best_a,
                        n, 2, 1.40,
                        phase_b_end, rng, 100
                    )
                    if np.isfinite(sc2p):
                        add_candidate(candidates, a2p, sc2p)
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"  mode2 polish failed n={n}: {e}")

    print(f"\n[phase done] candidates={len(candidates)}, t={time.monotonic()-t_start:.0f}s")

    # Pick a reasonable seed for black-box refinement.
    best_internal_score = np.inf
    best_internal_a = None
    for sc, a in candidates:
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_internal_score:
            best_internal_score = float(sc)
            best_internal_a = a

    if best_internal_a is None and parent is not None:
        best_internal_a = normalize_nonneg(parent)
    if best_internal_a is None:
        best_internal_a = np.ones(6962, dtype=np.float64) / 6962.0

    # Black-box refinement using the actual evaluator.
    holder = [np.inf, to_output_list(np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0, 1.0)]

    # Evaluate parent variants early to have a fallback in holder.
    if parent is not None:
        for target_sum in (1.0, max(1.0, min(float(parent.sum()), 20000.0))):
            try:
                lp = to_output_list(parent, target_sum)
                vp = eval_safe(lp)
                if np.isfinite(vp) and vp < holder[0]:
                    holder[0] = vp
                    holder[1] = lp
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Also evaluate a couple of simplest candidates before refinement.
    for sc, a in candidates[:8]:
        try:
            la = to_output_list(a, 1.0)
            va = eval_safe(la)
            if np.isfinite(va) and va < holder[0]:
                holder[0] = va
                holder[1] = la
        except Exception:
            pass

    print(f"[blackbox start] current best_actual={holder[0]:.8f}")

    if time.monotonic() < phase_c_end:
        blackbox_refine(best_internal_a, len(best_internal_a), rng, phase_c_end, holder)

    # Final sweep over all stored candidates and parent scales.
    evaluated = 0
    max_eval_final = 45
    for sc, a in candidates:
        if evaluated >= max_eval_final or time.monotonic() > deadline - 5.0:
            break
        try:
            la = to_output_list(a, 1.0)
            va = eval_safe(la)
            if np.isfinite(va) and va < holder[0]:
                holder[0] = va
                holder[1] = la
            evaluated += 1
        except Exception:
            pass

    if parent is not None:
        for target_sum in (1.0, max(1.0, min(float(parent.sum()), 20000.0)), 10.0):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5.0:
                break
            try:
                la = to_output_list(parent, target_sum)
                va = eval_safe(la)
                if np.isfinite(va) and va < holder[0]:
                    holder[0] = va
                    holder[1] = la
                evaluated += 1
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Last safety check.
    if holder[1] is None or not np.isfinite(holder[0]):
        fallback = to_output_list(np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64) / 4096.0, 1.0)
        vf = eval_safe(fallback)
        if np.isfinite(vf):
            holder[0] = vf
            holder[1] = fallback

    out = list(holder[1]) if len(holder[1]) > 0 else to_output_list(np.ones(1024.0), 1.0)
    print(f"[final] returned length={len(out)}, internal_best={best_internal_score:.8f}, actual_best={holder[0]:.8f}")
    return out
```
